## Summary
<h2>2026.9.7</h2>
<h3>Highlights</h3>
<ul>

<h3>Changes</h3>
<ul>
<li>Worktree sessions: start a new chat in an isolated managed worktree from web, iOS, or Android so parallel sessions on one repository no longer collide. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="4817744423" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/100788" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/100788/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/100788">#100788</a>) Thanks <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/obviyus/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/obviyus">@obviyus</a>.</li>
<li>Voice: Talk, Discord voice, FaceTime, and Twilio voice calls share one agent context, so voice answers about other sessions delegate to the agent instead of denying that work, and read-only lookups no longer ask for spoken confirmation. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5601202850" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/159548" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159548/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159548">#159548</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5600328037" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/159474" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159474/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159474">#159474</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5548418975" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/156151" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156151/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156151">#156151</a>) Thanks <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/obviyus/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/obviyus">@obviyus</a>, <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/VACInc/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/VACInc">@VACInc</a>, and <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/Peetiegonzalez/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/Peetiegonzalez">@Peetiegonzalez</a>.</li>
<li>Role model limits: administrators can give named operator roles a model policy that native agents, Visitor Access, model pickers, and plugin completions enforce, with denials keeping their documented authorization error code. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5550717288" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/156346" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156346/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156346">#156346</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5527902559" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/154893" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/154893/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/154893">#154893</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5527205732" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/154839" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/154839/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/154839">#154839</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5555627361" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/156604" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156604/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156604">#156604</a>) Thanks <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/shakkernerd/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/shakkernerd">@shakkernerd</a> and <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/obviyus/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/obviyus">@obviyus</a>.</li>
<li>Chat-driven setup: owners can hand their agent an API key, a config change, or an edit to a skill they own directly in chat, agents stop refusing or arguing over ordinary requested work while tool policy, sandboxing, and approvals still decide what needs confirmation, and OpenClaw change approvals now complete in the requesting chat with Allow once and Deny buttons or <code>/approve &lt;id&gt;</code>. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5583035513" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/158120" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158120/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158120">#158120</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5572162103" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/157447" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157447/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157447">#157447</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5578795491" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/157947" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157947/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157947">#157947</a>) Thanks <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/obviyus/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/obviyus">@obviyus</a>, <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/jalehman/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/jalehman">@jalehman</a>, and <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/joshavant/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/joshavant">@joshavant</a>.</li>
<li>Desktop audio: listen to managed desktop application sound from the docked desktop panel or the standalone desktop view, with isolated per-application audio on managed Linux desktops, playback tied to screen authorization, and setup warnings kept in a tooltip. <li>Session access: people with session-only grants can read visible conversations, receive updates, copy history as Markdown, and rename, pin, archive, and restore their own sessions, while other people's sessions stay read-only. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5532228771" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/155184" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/155184/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/155184">#155184</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5536628237" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/155565" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/155565/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/155565">#155565</a>) Thanks <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/shakkernerd/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/shakkernerd">@shakkernerd</a>.</li>
<li>Discord and Slack: accept bot-authored messages by default when <code>allowBots</code> is omitted, still subject to existing access and mention rules (explicit <code>false</code> and <code>"mentions"</code> are unchanged), and keep parent context when someone replies to a bot's Discord message. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5564221477" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/157091" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157091/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157091">#157091</a>)</li>
<li>People and presence: agents can use the <code>presence</code> tool to list who is online and which device produced a person's latest activity, idle people stay shown as online, administrators can merge duplicate profiles with <code>openclaw users merge</code>, and members of multi-user Gateways can edit their personal <code>USER.md</code> in Profile settings. Fixes <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5599724896" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/159423" data-hovercard-type="issue" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/issues/159423/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/159423">#159423</a> and <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5533374262" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/155255" data-hovercard-type="issue" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/issues/155255/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/155255">#155255</a>. <h3>Fixes</h3>
<ul>
<li>Older installs: Doctor recovers retained plugin sources that blocked Gateway startup after long upgrade jumps, migrates every agent database before repairs open it, resumes schema repair after an interrupted startup migration, and finishes state migration after legacy install repair. <li>Nodes and health: headless nodes with default plugins activate automatic updates, the health reader surfaces config read failures, and the progress card no longer lingers after dismissing an unfinished or note-only card. (#160790, #160885, #160867) Thanks @laurencebrown.</li>
<li>Docker updates: running-Gateway package updates on Docker and OverlayFS no longer spend many minutes retaining and retiring the previous runtime. (#160845)</li>
<li>Sessions and cloud workers: async transcript writes from stale owners are rejected, and node enrollment deadlines follow the bootstrap window. (#160893, #160904)</li>
</ul>
<h3>Known issues</h3>
<ul>
<li><strong>Windows updates from 2026.9.6:</strong> on Windows, updating from 2026.9.6 can exit silently with code 13; run <code>openclaw update</code> again.</li>
<li><strong>Docker update time:</strong> updating a Docker or OverlayFS installation is faster in this release but can still take several minutes, mostly while the Gateway is stopped for the package swap. (#160693)</li>
</ul>
<h3>Upcoming deprecations</h3>
<p>These deprecated compatibility paths remain available in <strong>2026.9.7</strong>. Their recorded removal targets are <strong>2026-09-30</strong> and <strong>2026-10-01</strong>; removal-pending entries stay until supported external plugin migration is verified. See the <a href="https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/sdk-migration" rel="nofollow">Plugin SDK migration guide</a> and <a href="https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/compatibility" rel="nofollow">plugin compatibility</a>.</p>
<ul>
<li><strong><code>openclaw/plugin-sdk/config-runtime</code>:</strong> use <code>api.pluginConfig</code>, <code>openclaw/plugin-sdk/config-mutation</code>, <code>openclaw/plugin-sdk/runtime-config-snapshot</code>, and <code>openclaw/plugin-sdk/config-contracts</code>. Removal pending after 2026-10-01. (<code>plugin-sdk-config-runtime-subpath</code>)</li>
<li><strong><code>openclaw/plugin-sdk/channel-reply-pipeline</code>, <code>channel-lifecycle</code>, and <code>channel-message</code>:</strong> use <code>openclaw/plugin-sdk/channel-outbound</code>, plus <code>openclaw/plugin-sdk/channel-inbound</code> for message helpers. Removal pending after 2026-10-01. (<code>plugin-sdk-channel-reply-pipeline-subpath</code>, <code>plugin-sdk-channel-lifecycle-subpath</code>, <code>plugin-sdk-channel-message-subpath</code>)</li>
<li><strong><code>openclaw/plugin-sdk/infra-runtime</code>:</strong> move to focused subpaths including <code>delivery-queue-runtime</code>, <code>diagnostic-runtime</code>, <code>error-runtime</code>, <code>exec-approvals-runtime</code>, <code>fetch-runtime</code>, and <code>ssrf-runtime</code>. Removal pending after 2026-10-01. (<code>plugin-sdk-infra-runtime-subpath</code>)</li>
<li><strong>Public media-understanding and memory-host-core SDK facades:</strong> register media understanding with <code>api.registerMediaUnderstandingProvider(...)</code> and use host-prepared memory prompts through <code>openclaw/plugin-sdk/core</code>. Removal pending after 2026-09-30; see <a href="https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/architecture" rel="nofollow">plugin architecture</a>. (<code>plugin-sdk-media-understanding-public-demotion</code>, <code>plugin-sdk-memory-host-core-public-demotion</code>)</li>
<li><strong>Legacy media projection:</strong> use ordered <code>MsgContext.media</code> / <code>InboundMediaFacts[]</code>, typed hook <code>media</code> and <code>originalMedia</code>, <code>Attachment*</code> template variables, and <code>openclaw/plugin-sdk/media-local-roots</code>. Removal target 2026-10-01. (<code>media-legacy-projection</code>)</li>
<li><strong>Plugin SDK compatibility aliases:</strong> move to plugin-local channel setup input fields, focused runtime subpaths instead of broad runtime barrels, provider-local helper APIs, MessagePresentation values and renderers, focused replacements named by each <code>@deprecated</code> annotation, <code>AgentHarnessAttemptResult.terminal</code> and <code>AgentHarnessDeliveryDefaults.visibleReplies</code>, canonical official-plugin exports, canonical memory tables with <code>getRuntimeConfig</code>, the namespaced plugin API, and manifest-owned plugin metadata. Removal target 2026-10-01. (<code>plugin-sdk-channel-setup-input-fields</code>, <code>plugin-sdk-broad-runtime-barrels</code>, <code>plugin-sdk-provider-owned-helper-shims</code>, <code>message-presentation-legacy-bridges</code>, <code>plugin-sdk-focused-compat-aliases</code>, <code>agent-harness-terminal-result-aliases</code>, <code>official-plugin-export-aliases</code>, <code>memory-host-compatibility-aliases</code>, <code>plugin-runtime-api-compat-aliases</code>, <code>plugin-provider-manifest-compat-aliases</code>). Thanks <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/TeaCup404/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/TeaCup404">@TeaCup404</a>.</li>
<li><strong>Channel webhook listener inputs:</strong> use <code>legacyWebhook</code> for listener config; Doctor migrates stored <code>webhookPort</code>/<code>webhookHost</code> and <code>webhook.port</code> inputs. Warnings started 2026-09-26. See <a href="https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/doctor/config-migrations#channel-webhook-listeners" rel="nofollow">config migrations</a>. (<code>channel-webhook-listener-config-inputs</code>)</li>
</ul>

## Why It Matters

<h3>Changes</h3>
<ul>
<li>Worktree sessions: start a new chat in an isolated managed worktree from web, iOS, or Android so parallel sessions on one repository no longer collide. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="4817744423" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/100788" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/100788/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/100788">#100788</a>) Thanks <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/obviyus/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/obviyus">@obviyus</a>.</li>
<li>Voice: Talk, Discord voice, FaceTime, and Twilio voice calls share one agent context, so voice answers about other sessions delegate to the agent instead of denying that work, and read-only lookups no longer ask for spoken confirmation. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5601202850" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/159548" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159548/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159548">#159548</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5600328037" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/159474" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159474/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159474">#159474</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5548418975" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/156151" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156151/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156151">#156151</a>) Thanks <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/obviyus/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/obviyus">@obviyus</a>, <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/VACInc/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/VACInc">@VACInc</a>, and <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/Peetiegonzalez/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/Peetiegonzalez">@Peetiegonzalez</a>.</li>
<li>Role model limits: administrators can give named operator roles a model policy that native agents, Visitor Access, model pickers, and plugin completions enforce, with denials keeping their documented authorization error code. <li>Session access: people with session-only grants can read visible conversations, receive updates, copy history as Markdown, and rename, pin, archive, and restore their own sessions, while other people's sessions stay read-only. Guests can now create and control their own sessions. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5532228771" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/155184" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/155184/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/155184">#155184</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5536628237" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/155565" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/155565/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/155565">#155565</a>) Thanks <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/shakkernerd/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/shakkernerd">@shakkernerd</a>.</li>
<li>Discord and Slack: accept bot-authored messages by default when <code>allowBots</code> is omitted, still subject to existing access and mention rules (explicit <code>false</code> and <code>"mentions"</code> are unchanged), and keep parent context when someone replies to a bot's Discord message. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5564221477" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/157091" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157091/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157091">#157091</a>)</li>
<li>People and presence: agents can use the <code>presence</code> tool to list who is online and which device produced a person's latest activity, idle people stay shown as online, administrators can merge duplicate profiles with <code>openclaw users merge</code>, and members of multi-user Gateways can edit their personal <code>USER.md</code> in Profile settings. Fixes <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5599724896" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/159423" data-hovercard-type="issue" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/issues/159423/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/159423">#159423</a> and <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5533374262" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/155255" data-hovercard-type="issue" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/issues/155255/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/155255">#155255</a>. <li>Steering: restore steering for every newer message when a follow-up is queued, advance the queue past more than one message per Stop, preserve steering and show queued input across reconnects, keep quoted replies sent during an active response, and stop queued timeouts or a faulty compaction probe from failing the active turn. <h3>Known issues</h3>
<ul>
<li><strong>Windows updates from 2026.9.6:</strong> on Windows, updating from 2026.9.6 can exit silently with code 13; run <code>openclaw update</code> again.</li>
<li><strong>Docker update time:</strong> updating a Docker or OverlayFS installation is faster in this release but can still take several minutes, mostly while the Gateway is stopped for the package swap. (#160693)</li>
</ul>
<h3>Upcoming deprecations</h3>
<p>These deprecated compatibility paths remain available in <strong>2026.9.7</strong>. Their recorded removal targets are <strong>2026-09-30</strong> and <strong>2026-10-01</strong>; removal-pending entries stay until supported external plugin migration is verified. See the <a href="https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/sdk-migration" rel="nofollow">Plugin SDK migration guide</a> and <a href="https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/compatibility" rel="nofollow">plugin compatibility</a>.</p>
<ul>
<li><strong><code>openclaw/plugin-sdk/config-runtime</code>:</strong> use <code>api.pluginConfig</code>, <code>openclaw/plugin-sdk/config-mutation</code>, <code>openclaw/plugin-sdk/runtime-config-snapshot</code>, and <code>openclaw/plugin-sdk/config-contracts</code>. Removal pending after 2026-10-01. (<code>plugin-sdk-config-runtime-subpath</code>)</li>
<li><strong><code>openclaw/plugin-sdk/channel-reply-pipeline</code>, <code>channel-lifecycle</code>, and <code>channel-message</code>:</strong> use <code>openclaw/plugin-sdk/channel-outbound</code>, plus <code>openclaw/plugin-sdk/channel-inbound</code> for message helpers. Removal pending after 2026-10-01. (<code>plugin-sdk-channel-reply-pipeline-subpath</code>, <code>plugin-sdk-channel-lifecycle-subpath</code>, <code>plugin-sdk-channel-message-subpath</code>)</li>
<li><strong><code>openclaw/plugin-sdk/infra-runtime</code>:</strong> move to focused subpaths including <code>delivery-queue-runtime</code>, <code>diagnostic-runtime</code>, <code>error-runtime</code>, <code>exec-approvals-runtime</code>, <code>fetch-runtime</code>, and <code>ssrf-runtime</code>. Removal pending after 2026-10-01. (<code>plugin-sdk-infra-runtime-subpath</code>)</li>
<li><strong>Public media-understanding and memory-host-core SDK facades:</strong> register media understanding with <code>api.registerMediaUnderstandingProvider(...)</code> and use host-prepared memory prompts through <code>openclaw/plugin-sdk/core</code>. Removal pending after 2026-09-30; see <a href="https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/architecture" rel="nofollow">plugin architecture</a>. (<code>plugin-sdk-media-understanding-public-demotion</code>, <code>plugin-sdk-memory-host-core-public-demotion</code>)</li>
<li><strong>Legacy media projection:</strong> use ordered <code>MsgContext.media</code> / <code>InboundMediaFacts[]</code>, typed hook <code>media</code> and <code>originalMedia</code>, <code>Attachment*</code> template variables, and <code>openclaw/plugin-sdk/media-local-roots</code>. Removal target 2026-10-01. (<code>media-legacy-projection</code>)</li>
<li><strong>Plugin SDK compatibility aliases:</strong> move to plugin-local channel setup input fields, focused runtime subpaths instead of broad runtime barrels, provider-local helper APIs, MessagePresentation values and renderers, focused replacements named by each <code>@deprecated</code> annotation, <code>AgentHarnessAttemptResult.terminal</code> and <code>AgentHarnessDeliveryDefaults.visibleReplies</code>, canonical official-plugin exports, canonical memory tables with <code>getRuntimeConfig</code>, the namespaced plugin API, and manifest-owned plugin metadata. Removal target 2026-10-01. (<code>plugin-sdk-channel-setup-input-fields</code>, <code>plugin-sdk-broad-runtime-barrels</code>, <code>plugin-sdk-provider-owned-helper-shims</code>, <code>message-presentation-legacy-bridges</code>, <code>plugin-sdk-focused-compat-aliases</code>, <code>agent-harness-terminal-result-aliases</code>, <code>official-plugin-export-aliases</code>, <code>memory-host-compatibility-aliases</code>, <code>plugin-runtime-api-compat-aliases</code>, <code>plugin-provider-manifest-compat-aliases</code>). Thanks <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/TeaCup404/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/TeaCup404">@TeaCup404</a>.</li>
<li><strong>Channel webhook listener inputs:</strong> use <code>legacyWebhook</code> for listener config; Doctor migrates stored <code>webhookPort</code>/<code>webhookHost</code> and <code>webhook.port</code> inputs. Warnings started 2026-09-26. See <a href="https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/doctor/config-migrations#channel-webhook-listeners" rel="nofollow">config migrations</a>. (<code>channel-webhook-listener-config-inputs</code>)</li>
</ul>
